  本项目特定的不可违反约束（GPU 设备号、服务器名、账号、路径限制等）。
  写本项目特定的，不要抄 CLAUDE.md 通用 L1 红线。

  - 服务器名 + 工作目录 + Python 路径 + conda env
  - 模型路径 / 数据集路径 / wandb config / HF cache

  - 等待中的 Worker 回调 / Cron / Director 决策

  格式 "条件 X 满足 → 动作 Y"。例如:
  - "Falcon-H1R 下载完成 → sed 修 MODEL_PATH → dry-run → 提交"
  - "pilot_011 出 GC4 结果 → ≥2pp 转架构 narrative，<2pp 写 null-result"

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
  - 项目核心结论 / 决策链（D001..）
  - 下一个"你"最容易判断错的事 / 容易踩的坑

    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Subsumption Test Results (50 samples, seq_len=2048)\\n\\n### 核心指标\\n- Linearity ratio: 0.264 ± 0.007 (cross-layer cascade non-linearity)\\n- Magnitude ratio: 0.158 ± 0.009 (SPON correction / predictor effect)\\n- Cosine similarity: 0.196 ± 0.027 (near-orthogonal)\\n\\n### Effect magnitudes\\n- ‖D(predictor)‖ = 482.65 (KL predictor logit shift)\\n- ‖D(SPON)‖ = 76.20 (SPON bias correction logit shift)\\n- ‖interaction‖ = 147.74 (cross-layer cascade non-linearity)\\n\\n### 结论：Informative — SPON corrections are orthogonal and negligibly small\\n\\n1. **SPON 与 mask 效应正交**：cos_sim ≈ 0.20，两种修正作用在不同维度\\n2. **SPON 量级微小**：仅为 predictor 效应的 16%（76 vs 483）\\n3. **逐层精确可加**：交互项（148）来源于跨层级联非线性（down_proj 是线性的，层内 mask+bias 严格可加），非层内交互\\n4. **支持 D010 \\\"marginal complement\\\" framing**：SPON 提供的是正交但微弱的补充，不构成有意义的 compositional gain\\n\\n⚠️ 不使用 \\\"subsumption\\\" 措辞——数据显示正交性而非包含关系。正确表述：SPON corrections are orthogonal to and much smaller than predictor mask effects; the non-zero interaction term arises from cross-layer cascading, not intra-layer coupling.\\n\\n### 产出文件\\nresults/subsumption_test/subsumption_results.json (15KB)\", \"completed_at\": \"2026-05-15T20:29:47.949Z\", \"completed_at\": \"2026-05-16T00:43:29.657Z\", \"notes\": \"## GSM8K 生成质量对比：Dense vs KL Sparse 50% (10 samples)\\n\\n### 结论：推理能力根本性丧失（模式 A），非仅数字算错\\n\\n| 维度 | Dense | Sparse 50% |\\n|------|-------|------------|\\n| 推理连贯性 | 多步推理，逻辑清晰 | 1-3 句后进入无限重复循环 |\\n| 正确答案 | 多数正确 | 0/10 |\\n| 重复退化 | 无 | 10/10 全部出现 |\\n| 拼写质量 | 正常 | 退化（crabs→crbs, skein→skeen）|\\n\\n### 退化模式\\nSparse 模型能生成与问题相关的开头（如 \\\"First, we need to know...\\\"），但随即进入同一句话的无限重复（15+ 次）。Sample 7 中正确算出 9×2=18，但无法继续推理。\\n\\n### 对论文的意义\\n50% sparsity 摧毁了深层推理能力。PPL-downstream disconnect 的机制：PPL 衡量 next-token 平均质量，repetition loop 的每个重复 token 仍有较高概率（模型\\\"自信地\\\"重复），但推理链断裂。\", \"parent\": \"lm_eval_kl_s42\"}, \"d030_kl40_uniform\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"retry_count\": 0, \"created_at\": \"account\": \"root\", \"wall_clock_seconds\": 2215.3, \"gpu_hours\": 0.62}, \"notes\": \"TEAL 50% downstream 结果确认 collapse 是 universal：\\n\\n| Task | TEAL 50% | KL 50% | Dense | TEAL drop | KL drop |\\n|------|----------|--------|-------|-----------|---------|\\n| arc_challenge (acc_norm) | 0.2910 | 0.3771 | 0.5819 | -50.0% | -35.2% |\\n| winogrande (acc) | 0.5556 | 0.6156 | 0.7814 | -28.9% | -21.2% |\\n| mmlu (acc) | 0.2856 | 0.2591 | 0.6532 | -56.3% | -60.3% |\\n| gsm8k (flexible-extract) | 0.0190 | 0.0121 | 0.5004 | -96.2% | -97.6% |\\n\\n关键发现：\\n1. TEAL 和 KL 在 MMLU/GSM8K 上均出现灾难性崩溃（接近随机水平），证明 50% sparsity downstream collapse 不是 KL predictor 特有问题\\n2. TEAL 在 ARC/WinoGrande 上甚至比 KL 更差（TEAL drop -50%/-29% vs KL drop -35%/-21%），说明 training-free 方法在简单 task 上也不如 learned predictor\\n3. MMLU/GSM8K 两者均近随机（MMLU ~0.25-0.29 = 4-choice random level），collapse 程度相当\\n4. 结论：50% unstructured sparsity 对 SwiGLU FFN 的 downstream capability 有根本性破坏，与 sparsity 选择方法无关\", \"reproduce\": distill_sparse_swiglu\\n\\n## 决策记录\\n\\n### 图表安排 (2026-05-18)\\n- mask_divergence_heatmap + neuron_scatter_layer16 → 正文\\n- mask_comparison (L2/16/28) → 附录\\n- 风格统一留给 Paper Agent 整合阶段\\n\\n### Latency Benchmark Reframe (2026-05-18)\\n- TEAL 无 sparse kernel（纯 mask-and-multiply），不能 claim wall-clock speedup\\n- Reframe 为 predictor overhead characterization（目标 < 5%）\\n- 论文报告 theoretical FLOP reduction + 实测 predictor overhead\\n- 明确标注 \\\"actual speedup requires sparse kernel integration → future work\\\"\\n- latency_benchmark 最终数据: predictor overhead 3.17ms (2.7%), compensation 3.3ms\\n\\n### Worker 幻觉教训 (2026-05-18)\\n- 分析 Worker 曾虚构 KL 30% 参考数字（MMLU=62.68%, GSM8K=38.06%），导致 downstream \\\"崩塌\\\" 假警报\\n- 教训：分析 Worker 必须约束为只从 registry 读取数据，禁止凭记忆引用数字\\n\\n### C1 s123 Oracle 错误 (2026-05-18)\\n- c1_ablation_bce_kl_targets_s123 使用了错误的 oracle（kl_sparsity30_s123 = 30%）而非正确的 mvp_kl_norm_v2_s123 (50%)\\n- v2 已用正确 checkpoint 重新提交并确认\\n- 教训：提交实验前必须交叉验证 checkpoint 路径与 hypothesis 中的 sparsity level 一致\\n\\n### GPU 参数教训 (2026-05-18)\\n- CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=N 使得脚本内只看到一张卡 → 必须用 --gpu 0（第一张可见卡）\\n- submit_training_job 自动注入 CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES，脚本内 --gpu 始终为 0\\n\\n### Mask Visualization 最终数据 (2026-05-18, 64 samples)\\n- **论文必须引用 64-sample 数据，非 16-sample 初步数据**\\n- 50% sparsity: Jaccard distance = 0.682 (similarity = 0.318)\\n- 30% sparsity: Jaccard distance = 0.463 (similarity = 0.537)\\n- 30% 深层 (L24-L28) divergence 最大: Jaccard distance 0.55-0.60\\n- 30% 中间层 (L15-L18) agreement 最高: Jaccard sim ~0.61-0.63\\n- Sparsity 越高 → mask 分歧越大（30%→0.46, 50%→0.68）\\n\\n### C1 因果消融最终确认 (2026-05-18)\\n- s42: PPL=9.29, sparsity=0.609, agreement=0.978\\n- s123_v2: PPL=9.32, sparsity=0.615, agreement=0.979\\n- TEAL 50% uniform downstream eval: PPL=11.964, 与 KL (11.963) max Δ = 0.59pp\\n- 结论：target quality 是 KL 优越性的唯一来源，gradient coupling 无独立贡献\\n\\n### BCE 30% 结果 (2026-05-18)\\n- PPL=11.24 vs KL=8.98 (Δ=2.27)\\n- Downstream: BCE ARC=41.2%, MMLU=36.3%, GSM8K=6.0%\\n- 确认 KL mask 本质上优于 BCE mask\\n\\n## 环境约束\\n\\n### 服务器\\n- westd-34887: 2×A6000, /root/distill_sparse_swiglu/, checkpoints 较全\\n- westd-41718: 2×GPU, checkpoints 包含 kl_sparsity25/30 和 mvp_kl_norm_v2, c1_ablation 系列\\n- HF 离线模式必须: HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1 TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE=1 HF_HOME=/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache\\n- 模型路径: /root/autodl-tmp/models/llama-3.1-8b\\n\\n### Predictor 参数\\n- GELU 激活, d_b=128, {"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_14/projects/distill_sparse_swiglu/paper_framing.md\", \"content\": \"# Paper Framing — distill_sparse_swiglu\\n\\n## Target Venue\\n- Primary: EMNLP 2026 Main (ARR May 2026 cycle)\\n- Deadline: 2026-05-25 (ARR submission)\\n- Track: Efficient Methods for NLP\\n\\n## Core Claims\\n\\n1. **KL distillation discovers fundamentally different (and superior) neuron importance patterns that magnitude-based oracles miss**: The C1 causal ablation (D059) proves that KL's advantage comes **entirely** from target quality (which neurons to keep), with gradient coupling contributing ≈0 across **both PPL and all downstream tasks**. **Controlled eval (TEAL 50% uniform, matched protocol)**: BCE+KL-targets PPL = **11.9638**, KL predictor PPL = **11.9638** (exact match). **Downstream confirmation (full-spectrum)**: All 4 downstream tasks (ARC-c, WinoGrande, MMLU, GSM8K) also match within <1pp delta, confirming gradient coupling = 0 across PPL + reasoning + knowledge + math dimensions. Standard BCE (magnitude masks) PPL ≈ 22.51. Target quality alone accounts for KL's full advantage. *Additional evidence from training eval (Gumbel hard mask)*: BCE+KL-targets PPL 9.29 @60.9% ≈ KL 9.21 @60.6%, consistent with controlled eval. The 53% mask divergence between KL-derived and magnitude-based masks is the core empirical finding: KL discovers a qualitatively different neuron importance landscape. **Implication**: The gradient coupling analysis (§3.6) provides mathematical intuition for *why* KL can discover better targets (coupled gradients explore joint neuron interaction patterns), but the causal driver is the resulting mask quality, not the gradient pathway per se. This shifts the contribution from \\\"engineering a better loss\\\" to \\\"discovering that output-distribution distillation reveals hidden neuron importance structure.\\\" **Supporting evidence**: (a) Iso-compute ablation: BCE with 2.71× more FLOPs still PPL=27.97 vs KL=12.00, confirming the gap is structural; (b) BCE at recommended 30% operating point (D062): forced TEAL top-k PPL=11.24 vs KL=8.82, Δ=2.42 — BCE 30% is nearly as bad as KL 50% (11.80), meaning BCE's inferior masks waste ~20pp of sparsity budget; (c) BCE Lagrangian failure at 30%: BCE loss scale (~0.6) vs KL (~0.0001) causes same Lagrangian to fail; (d) No compensation needed: KL eliminates 67.2M compensation parameters (~256MB). **Anticipated attack**: \\\"Two-stage training (KL→oracle→BCE) avoids needing KL predictor at inference?\\\" Defense: KL predictor is contextual (per-input adaptive); oracle masks are fixed. Deployment requires per-input sparsity adaptation. **Remaining caveat**: Single seed (s42); C1 s123 in progress for statistical robustness. (Headline claim, resolves persistent R3-R8 confound)\\n\\n2. **SPON's static correction is orthogonal to KL and too weak to complement it**: Logit-space decomposition (plan_005) shows SPON's bias correction is near-orthogonal to KL's mask effect (cos_sim=0.196, per-layer ≤0) with only 15.8% relative magnitude. Cross-layer interaction is non-trivial (30.6%) but PPL recovery remains modest (7.3%, 0.23 PPL). Rather than KL \\\"subsuming\\\" SPON, the two target different error components—but SPON's component is too small to provide meaningful complementary benefit. Strengthens Claim 1: static correction is not just redundant but irrelevant to the dominant error mode that KL addresses. (Evidence: plan_005_subsumption_s42, spon_2048_s42, D009, D010)\\n\\n3. **Sparsity regime determines whether allocation or capacity is the bottleneck (phase transition finding)**: Empirical discovery from D030 uniform allocation experiments reveals a qualitative phase transition, not the originally predicted quadratic scaling. At ≤30% sparsity, global top-k causes high seed-to-seed variance in downstream metrics due to per-layer allocation imbalance (uniform allocation stabilizes MMLU: σ drops from 9.5pp to 1.3pp across 3 seeds, mean 40.7% vs TEAL's 38.6%). At ≥50%, model capacity itself is insufficient — no allocation strategy can recover (uniform MMLU=27.8%≈random). The transition reveals three distinct regimes with non-monotonic allocation preference: (1) **Budget-surplus regime (≤30%)**: uniform allocation reduces seed-to-seed instability by 7.5× (σ: 1.3pp vs 9.5pp across 3 seeds), stabilizing downstream performance (3-seed mean MMLU: 40.7±1.3pp uniform vs 35.7±9.5pp global); (2) **Budget-constrained regime (~40%)**: learned allocation outperforms blind uniformity — MMLU 38.1% (global) vs 30.7% (uniform), +7.4pp — the predictor's learned priorities become valuable when budget is tight; (3) **Capacity-cliff regime (≥50%)**: allocation irrelevant, both collapse to ~28% ≈ random. Recommended operating point (8B-validated): 30% sparsity with uniform allocation. At 30%, KL uniform dominates on the efficiency-quality Pareto frontier: PPL 8.82 vs TEAL 10.97 (−19.6%), with comparable-to-better downstream (MMLU +2.0pp, WG +2.5pp; ARC-c −1.5pp, GSM8K −0.5pp within noise). The advantage is primarily in PPL and knowledge-intensive tasks, not uniform across all benchmarks. **14B scale-dependent caveat (D054)**: At Qwen-2.5-14B 50% sparsity, uniform allocation does NOT fix KL's MMLU gap (64.49% uniform vs 63.72% global, only +0.77pp; TEAL global=71.83%). The 8B allocation-dependent pattern does not generalize to 14B. This is a valuable negative finding revealing loss × allocation × scale three-way interaction. 14B claim scope: KL PPL advantage preserved (7.62 vs 8.73 TEAL), but downstream MMLU gap persists regardless of allocation. The non-monotonic allocation preference (uniform@30% → global@40% → irrelevant@50%) provides mechanistic insight: at generous budgets, ensuring no layer is starved matters most; at tight budgets, the predictor's learned cross-layer priorities add value; at catastrophic budgets, capacity itself is the binding constraint. The failure mode changes qualitatively: allocation-fixable (≤30%) vs capacity-limited (≥50%). PPL non-monotonicity (40%=8.36 < 30%=8.82) while downstream is strictly monotonic further supports \\\"PPL is a poor proxy for downstream quality\\\" narrative. **6-point PPL regime curve (D063)**: 30%=8.82, 35%=9.31, 40%=10.12, 45%=10.80, 50%=11.80 (TEAL global). Smooth monotonic increase from 30-50%, providing dense sampling for regime boundary identification. 25% eval pending. This yields a practical deployment guideline and a mechanistic understanding of when/why sparse inference degrades. (Evidence: d030_kl30_uniform, d030_kl50_uniform, d030_kl40_uniform, bench_teal30, bench_teal50, bench_kl30_global, bench_kl50_global, lm_eval downstream series)\\n\\n## Story Spine\\n\\n- **Problem**: SwiGLU activation sparsity enables 40-60% neuron pruning at inference, but training-free heuristics (WINA/TEAL) leave a 6+ point quality gap, while learned BCE predictors require auxiliary compensation networks adding ~256MB overhead (FastForward).\\n\\n- **Existing approaches & gaps**: (a) Training-free (WINA, TEAL, R-Sparse): no training cost, but quality gap too large for deployment. (b) Static learned (SPON): zero-overhead bias correction via KL distillation, but input-independent — cannot adapt to per-input neuron importance. (c) Contextual learned (FastForward): BCE predictor + compensation MLP, near-dense quality but inference overhead from compensation network. Gap: no prior work trains the contextual predictor itself with KL, and no work quantifies how much static correction remains useful once a coupled-loss contextual predictor is in place.\\n\\n- **Our framing / headline question**: \\\"What does KL distillation actually learn for SwiGLU sparsity prediction, and why do its masks differ fundamentally from magnitude-based oracles?\\\" The theoretical grounding (§3.6, suggestive): BCE gradient dL_BCE/ds_j depends only on neuron j (factorized), while KL gradient dL_KL/ds_j couples all active neurons through d_logits/dh * dh/dm. This provides intuition for how KL *can* discover joint interaction patterns. The C1 causal ablation (§4.4) then empirically confirms: the masks KL discovers, not the gradient pathway itself, are what matter.\\n\\n- **Headlin\\n\\n... - id: D070
  date: 2026-05-19 02:59 UTC
  summary: Accept 冲刺计划修正：砍 LLaMA-3-8B（同一家族）和 sparse kernel（高风险+叙事矛盾），改为聚焦 Mistral
    TEAL baseline + C1 多种子 + 写作质量
  rationale: Reviewer 指出主模型是 LLaMA-3.1-8B，加 LLaMA-3-8B 是同一家族，审稿人不认可。sparse kernel
    与刚完成的 P2 去部署定位矛盾，且 6 天内从零实现可靠 benchmark 风险过高。真正的关键缺口是 Mistral TEAL 30% downstream
    baseline（没有对照，Mistral downstream 数据无法 contextualize）。调整后优先级：P0 Mistral TEAL baseline,
    P0 C1 多种子（已在跑），P1 写作整合。
  related_experiments:
  - exp_id: mistral_kl30_downstream
    role: trigger
    result: Mistral MMLU=44.04% GSM8K=11.14% 退化严重，需 TEAL baseline 对比
  depends_on:
  - D069
- id: D071
  date: 2026-05-19 05:07 UTC
  summary: Mistral TEAL baseline 确认 KL cross-arch downstream 优势：ARC +9pp, WG +5.6pp。C1
    retrain max Δ=1.70pp。Cross-arch 从弱点变为加分项。
  rationale: Mistral TEAL 30% downstream 结果出炉，KL 在 4/5 tasks 上优于 TEAL（ARC +9.0pp,
    WG +5.6pp, PPL -1.61, MMLU +0.6pp），仅 GSM8K TEAL 略好（低绝对值区间）。50% TEAL 也崩溃（MMLU≈随机），capacity
    cliff 跨架构成立。C1 retrain 使用 kl_30pct oracles 后 max Δ=1.70pp，消除 checkpoint 不一致攻击面。论文
    cross-arch claim 可从 PPL-only 升级为 downstream-supported。
  related_experiments:
  - exp_id: mistral_teal30_downstream
    role: key_evidence
    result: ARC=41.1% WG=63.5% MMLU=43.5% GSM8K=15.9%
  - exp_id: mistral_teal50_downstream
    role: supporting
    result: MMLU=26.4% GSM8K=3.4% capacity cliff confirmed
  - exp_id: eval_c1_retrain_30pct_s123
    role: supporting
    result: MMLU=39.9% vs KL 39.4% delta=0.48pp
  - exp_id: eval_c1_retrain_30pct_s456
    role: supporting
    result: MMLU=41.0% vs KL 42.0% delta=-0.94pp
  depends_on:
  - D070
- id: D072
  date: 2026-05-19 07:18 UTC
  summary: 'Round 11 Action Matrix: C1 reframe (mask transferability), Abstract 30%-first,
    14B diagnostic analysis, BCE+Gumbel no-penalty experiment'
  rationale: 'Round 11 审稿 3.0 Findings。三个 critical: (1) C1 推断逻辑混淆 mask transferability
    与 gradient coupling unimportance — reframe 为 "coupled gradients discover superior
    masks that transfer to simpler objectives"; (2) 14B MMLU 反转缺 mechanism — 14B mask
    divergence 分析显示 layer heterogeneity 2.4x higher (Jaccard std 0.076 vs 0.031),
    early-layer convergence zone 提供假说; (3) 49% PPL headline 来自 collapsed regime —
    Abstract/Intro 重构以 30% 为主。C1 Table 3 s42 使用旧 oracle 导致 max Δ=7.85pp 异常，排除后 retrained
    seeds max Δ=1.18pp。'
  related_experiments:
  - exp_id: qwen14b_mask_divergence_analysis
    role: evidence
    result: 14B Jaccard std 0.076 vs 8B 0.031, L4=0.063 convergence zone
  - exp_id: teal_28.5pct_sanity
    role: sanity_check
    result: PPL=10.30, KL still 13% better at matched sparsity
  - exp_id: bce_gumbel_no_penalty_s42
    role: control
    result: running
  depends_on:
  - D071
- id: D073
  date: 2026-05-19 08:19 UTC
  summary: 'Round 12 Action Matrix: Fix N1/N2 data errors, delete BCE+Comp row, rename
    C1 to target-swap, 14B multi-seed, soften taxonomy'
  rationale: Round 12 审稿 3.0 Findings。两个 critical 数值错误：(1) C1 50% max Δ=0.59 应为 0.79,
    1-seed not 2-seed; (2) Table 2 "BCE+comp" 实际无 compensation（标签虚假），Table 4 BCE+comp
    用了 per-token eval 非 TEAL（protocol 不一致）。处理：修正 N1 数字，Table 2 改标签，Table 4 删除 BCE+Comp
    行。C1 改名 "Target-Swap Ablation"。14B 补跑 s123+s456（98GB VRAM 可行）。MSE baseline 无现成代码不跑，软化
    taxonomy。
  related_experiments:
  - exp_id: bce_gumbel_no_penalty_s42

    result: PPL=32.22, loss function is primary driver
